- **rt-reporting**: Write red-team / penetration-test findings that survive scrutiny: a reproducible finding structure (what, where, impact bound, evidence, falsifier), severity derived from demonstrated reachability and impact rather than scanner labels, remediation by root cause and detection guidance, and an executive summary that does not overclaim.
